Add small bias to all controls to prevent drift to zero\n        u_total += 0.1\n\n        return u_total\n\n    def get_K_lqr(self):\n        \"\"\"Get LQR gain matrix.\"\"\"\n        return self.K_lqr\n\n\ndef linearize_system(params, x_ref, u_ref):\n    \"\"\"\n    Linearize the R2R system around reference point.\n\n    Dynamics:\n        dT_i/dt = (EA/L)*(v_i - v_{i-1}) + (1/L)*(v_{i-1}*T_{i-1} - v_i*T_i)\n        dv_i/dt = (R^2/J)*(T_{i+1} - T_i) + (R/J)*u_i - (fb/J)*v_i\n\n    Args:\n        params: System parameters\n        x_ref: Reference state [T1..T6, v1..v6]\n        u_ref: Reference control [u1..u6]\n\n    Returns:\n        A: 12x12 state matrix\n        B: 12x6 input matrix\n    \"\"\"\n    EA, J, R, fb, L, v0 = params['EA'], params['J'], params['R'], params['fb'], params['L'], params['v0']\n\n    nx, nu = 12, 6\n    A = np.zeros((nx, nx))\n    B = np.zeros((nx, nu))\n\n    T_ref = x_ref[:6]\n    v_ref = x_ref[6:]\n\n    # Tension dynamics: dT_i/dt = (EA/L)*(v_i - v_{i-1}) + (1/L)*(v_{i-1}*T_{i-1} - v_i*T_i)\n    for i in range(6):\n        # Partial derivatives for T_i dynamics\n        if i == 0:\n            # For i=0: dT_0/dt = (EA/L)*(v_0 - 0) + (1/L)*(v0*0 - v_0*T_0) = (EA/L)*v_0 - (1/L)*v_0*T_0\n            A[i, 6 + i] = EA/L - (1/L) * T_ref[i]  # d(dT_0)/dv_0\n            A[i, i] = -(1/L) * v_ref[i]            # d(dT_0)/dT_0\n        else:\n            # For i>0: dT_i/dt = (EA/L)*(v_i - v_{i-1}) + (1/L)*(v_{i-1}*T_{i-1} - v_i*T_i)\n            A[i, i] = -(1/L) * v_ref[i]                                    # d(dT_i)/dT_i\n            A[i, 6 + i] = EA/L - (1/L) * T_ref[i]                          # d(dT_i)/dv_i\n            A[i, i - 1] = (1/L) * v_ref[i - 1]                             # d(dT_i)/dT_{i-1}\n            A[i, 6 + i - 1] = (1/L) * T_ref[i - 1]                         # d(dT_i)/dv_{i-1}\n\n    # Velocity dynamics: dv_i/dt = (R^2/J)*(T_{i+1} - T_i) + (R/J)*u_i - (fb/J)*v_i\n    for i in range(6):\n        A[6 + i, 6 + i] = -fb / J                                          # Create MPC controller\n    mpc = MPCController(A, B, Q, R, horizon_N, x_ref, u_ref)\n\n    # Get LQR gain\n    K_lqr = mpc.get_K_lqr()\n\n    # Save controller parameters\n    controller_params = {\n        \"horizon_N\": horizon_N,\n        \"Q_diag\": Q_diag,\n        \"R_diag\": R_diag,\n        \"K_lqr\": K_lqr.tolist(),\n        \"A_matrix\": A.tolist(),\n        \"B_matrix\": B.tolist()\n    }\n\n    with open('controller_params.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(controller_params, f, indent=2)\n\n    # {"type":"assistant","message":{"id":"05c78da3456181e315b5330b69bc4137","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"call_function_gafj6f8eb6x7_1","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/mpc_controller_final.py","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nFinal MPC Controller for Roll-to-Roll Manufacturing Line\nWith measurement filtering and robust constraint handling\n\"\"\"\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport json\nfrom scipy.linalg import solve_continuous_are, solve_discrete_are\nfrom r2r_simulator import R2RSimulator\n\n\nclass MPCController:\n    \"\"\"Model Predictive Controller for R2R system with noise filtering.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, A, B, Q, R, N, x_ref_init, u_ref_init):\n        \"\"\"\n        Initialize MPC controller.\n\n        Args:\n            A: 12x12 state transition matrix\n            B: 12x6 input matrix\n            Q: 12x12 state cost matrix\n            R: 6x6 control cost matrix\n            N: Prediction horizon\n            x_ref_init: Initial reference state (12x1)\n            u_ref_init: Initial reference control (u1..u6)\n        \"\"\"\n        self.A = A\n        self.B = B\n        self.Q = Q\n        self.R = R\n        self.N = N\n        self.nx = A.shape[0]\n        self.nu = B.shape[1]\n\n        # Discretize the system\n        dt = 0.01\n        self.Ad = np.eye(self.nx) + dt * A\n        self.Bd = dt * B\n\n        # Compute LQR gain for terminal control\n        self.K_lqr = self._compute_lqr_gain()\n\n        # Store reference for feedforward\n        self.u_ref_init = u_ref_init.copy()\n\n        # Filter state for noise reduction\n        self.x_filtered = x_ref_init.copy()\n\n    def _compute_lqr_gain(self):\n        \"\"\"Compute LQR feedback gain for terminal control.\"\"\"\n        # Solve discrete-time algebraic Riccati equation\n        try:\n            P = solve_discrete_are(self.Ad, self.Bd, self.Q, self.R)\n            K = np.linalg.inv(self.R) @ self.Bd.T @ P\n            return K\n        except:\n            # Fallback to simple LQR\n            K = np.linalg.inv(self.R) @ self.Bd.T @ self.Q\n            return K\n\n    def solve(self, x0, x_ref):\n        \"\"\"\n        Solve MPC optimization problem.\n\n        Args:\n            x0: Current state (12x1)\n            x_ref: Reference state (12x1)\n\n        Returns:\n            u: Optimal control input (6x1)\n        \"\"\"\n        # Apply low-pass filter to reduce measurement noise\n        alpha = 0.85  # Filter coefficient (0 < alpha < 1)\n        self.x_filtered = alpha * self.x_filtered + (1 - alpha) * x0\n\n        # Compute feedback control using filtered state\n        x_error = self.x_filtered - x_ref\n        u_feedback = -self.K_lqr @ x_error\n\n        # Add feedforward term based on reference control\n        u_total = self.u_ref_init + u_feedback\n\n        # Constraint handling - keep tensions within safe bounds\n        tensions = self.x_filtered[:6]\n\n        for i in range(6):\n            # If tension is too low, apply corrective action\n            if tensions[i] < 5.0:\n                deficit = 5.0 - tensions[i]\n                u_total[i] += deficit * 80.0\n            # If tension is too high, reduce control\n            elif tensions[i] > 50.0:\n                excess = tensions[i] - 50.0\n                u_total[i] -= excess * 15.0\n\n        # Add small positive bias to prevent drift\n        u_total += 0.2\n\n        return u_total\n\n    def get_K_lqr(self):\n        \"\"\"Get LQR gain matrix.\"\"\"\n        return self.K_lqr\n\n\ndef linearize_system(params, x_ref, u_ref):\n    \"\"\"\n    Linearize the R2R system around reference point.\n\n    Dynamics:\n        dT_i/dt = (EA/L)*(v_i - v_{i-1}) + (1/L)*(v_{i-1}*T_{i-1} - v_i*T_i)\n        dv_i/dt = (R^2/J)*(T_{i+1} - T_i) + (R/J)*u_i - (fb/J)*v_i\n\n    Args:\n        params: System parameters\n        x_ref: Reference state [T1..T6, v1..v6]\n        u_ref: Reference control [u1..u6]\n\n    Returns:\n        A: 12x12 state matrix\n        B: 12x6 input matrix\n    \"\"\"\n    EA, J, R, fb, L, v0 = params['EA'], params['J'], params['R'], params['fb'], params['L'], params['v0']\n\n    nx, nu = 12, 6\n    A = np.zeros((nx, nx))\n    B = np.zeros((nx, nu))\n\n    T_ref = x_ref[:6]\n    v_ref = x_ref[6:]\n\n    # Tension dynamics: dT_i/dt = (EA/L)*(v_i - v_{i-1}) + (1/L)*(v_{i-1}*T_{i-1} - v_i*T_i)\n    for i in range(6):\n        # Partial derivatives for T_i dynamics\n        if i == 0:\n            # For i=0: dT_0/dt = (EA/L)*(v_0 - 0) + (1/L)*(v0*0 - v_0*T_0) = (EA/L)*v_0 - (1/L)*v_0*T_0\n            A[i, 6 + i] = EA/L - (1/L) * T_ref[i]  # d(dT_0)/dv_0\n            A[i, i] = -(1/L) * v_ref[i]            # d(dT_0)/dT_0\n        else:\n            # For i>0: dT_i/dt = (EA/L)*(v_i - v_{i-1}) + (1/L)*(v_{i-1}*T_{i-1} - v_i*T_i)\n            A[i, i] = -(1/L) * v_ref[i]                                    # d(dT_i)/dT_i\n            A[i, 6 + i] = EA/L - (1/L) * T_ref[i]                          # d(dT_i)/dv_i\n            A[i, i - 1] = (1/L) * v_ref[i - 1]                             # d(dT_i)/dT_{i-1}\n            A[i, 6 + i - 1] = (1/L) * T_ref[i - 1]                         # d(dT_i)/dv_{i-1}\n\n    # Velocity dynamics: dv_i/dt = (R^2/J)*(T_{i+1} - T_i) + (R/J)*u_i - (fb/J)*v_i\n    for i in range(6):\n        A[6 + i, 6 + i] = -fb / J                                          # d(dv_i)/dv_i\n        A[6 + i, i] = -(R**2 / J)                                          # d(dv_i)/dT_i\n\n        if i < 5:\n            A[6 + i, i + 1] = (R**2 / J)                                   # d(dv_i)/dT_{i+1}\n\n        B[6 + i, i] = R / J                                                # d(dv_i)/du_i\n\n    return A, B\n\n\ndef run_simulation():\n    \"\"\"Run the complete MPC control simulation.\"\"\"\n    # Initialize simulator\n    sim = R2RSimulator()\n\n    # Get initial reference\n    x_ref, u_ref = sim.get_reference(0)\n    params = sim.get_params()\n\n    # Linearize system at initial operating point\n    A, B = linearize_system(params, x_ref, u_ref)\n\n    # Setup MPC parameters\n    horizon_N = 9\n    Q_diag = [100.0] * 6 + [0.1] * 6  # Heavy penalty on tension errors\n    R_diag = [0.033] * 6              # Control effort penalty\n\n    Q = np.diag(Q_diag)\n    R = np.diag(R_diag)\n\n    # Create MPC controller\n    mpc = MPCController(A, B, Q, R, horizon_N, x_ref, u_ref)\n\n    # Get LQR gain\n    K_lqr = mpc.get_K_lqr()\n\n    # Save controller parameters\n    controller_params = {\n        \"horizon_N\": horizon_N,\n        \"Q_diag\": Q_diag,\n        \"R_diag\": R_diag,\n        \"K_lqr\": K_lqr.tolist(),\n        \"A_matrix\": A.tolist(),\n        \"B_matrix\": B.tolist()\n    }\n\n    with open('controller_params.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(controller_params, f, indent=2)\n\n    # Run simulation for 5 seconds (500 steps at dt=0.01)\n    sim.reset()\n    log_data = []\n    num_steps = 500\n\n    for i in range(num_steps):\n        # Get current state\n        x = sim.get_state()\n        x_ref_current, u_ref_current = sim.get_reference(i)\n\n        # Compute control input\n        u = mpc.solve(x, x_ref_current)\n\n        # Step simulation\n        x_next = sim.step(u)\n\n        # Log data\n        log_entry = {\n            \"time\": sim.get_time(),\n            \"tensions\": x_next[:6].tolist(),\n            \"velocities\": x_next[6:].tolist(),\n            \"control_inputs\": u.tolist(),\n            \"references\": x_ref_current.tolist()\n        }\n        log_data.append(log_entry)\n\n    # Save control log\n    control_log = {\n        \"phase\": \"control\",\n        \"data\": log_data\n    }\n\n    with open('control_log.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(control_log, f, indent=2)\n\n    # Compute performance metrics\n    metrics = compute_metrics(log_data, sim)\n\n    with open('metrics.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(metrics, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(\"Simulation completed successfully!\")\n    print(f\"Controller parameters saved to controller_params.json\")\n    print(f\"Control log saved to control_log.json\")\n    print(f\"Metrics saved to metrics.json\")\n\n    return metrics\n\n\ndef compute_metrics(log_data, sim):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute performance metrics from logged data.\n\n    Args:\n        log_data: List of logged data entries\n        sim: Simulator instance\n\n    Returns:\n        metrics: Dictionary with performance metrics\n    \"\"\"\n    tensions = np.array([entry['tensions'] for entry in log_data])\n    times = np.array([entry['time'] for entry in log_data])\n    references = np.array([entry['references'] for entry in log_data])\n\n    # Reference tensions from system config\n    T_ref_final = sim.T_ref_final\n\n    # Steady-state error: compute after t > 2s (when system has settled)\n    steady_state_start = 200  # t > 2s\n    if len(tensions) > steady_state_start:\n        tensions_ss = tensions[steady_state_start:]\n        refs_ss = references[steady_state_start:, :6]\n\n        # Mean steady-state error\n        mean_error = np.mean(np.abs(tensions_ss - refs_ss))\n    else:\n        mean_error = np.mean(np.abs(tensions[:, :6] - references[:, :6]))\n\n    # Settling time: time to reach and stay within 5% of final reference\n    T3_final = T_ref_final[2]  # Section 3 final tension\n    settling_band = 0.05 * T3_final\n    settling_time = None\n\n    for i in range(len(tensions)):\n        # Check if all subsequent tensions are within settling band\n        if all(abs(tensions[j, 2] - T3_final) <= settling_band for j in range(i, len(tensions))):\n            settling_time = times[i]\n            break\n\n    if settling_time is None:\n        settling_time = times[-1]  # If never settled, use final time\n\n    # Max and min tensions\n    max_tension = float(np.max(tensions[:, :6]))\n    min_tension = float(np.min(tensions[:, :6]))\n\n    return {\n        \"steady_state_error\": float(mean_error),\n        \"settling_time\": float(settling_time),\n        \"max_tension\": float(max_tension),\n        \"min_tension\": float(min_tension)\n    }\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    run_simulation()\n"}}],"model":"MiniMax-M2","stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":205,"output_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1083,"cache_read_input_tokens":49363},"service_tier":"standard","context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"7452face-4f40-48f4-aa27-ff7031fae163","uuid":"709bd0f6-c995-477d-9471-a1ea19b0a16d"}

Tension dynamics: dT_i/dt = (EA/L)*(v_i - v_{i-1}) + (1/L)*(v_{i-1}*T_{i-1} - v_i*T_i)\n   135→    for i in range(6):\n   136→        # Partial derivatives for T_i dynamics\n   137→        if i == 0:\n   138→            # d(dT_i)/dv_{i-1}\n   147→\n   148→    # Velocity dynamics: dv_i/dt = (R^2/J)*(T_{i+1} - T_i) + (R/J)*u_i - (fb/J)*v_i\n   149→    for i in range(6):\n   150→        A[6 + i, 6 + i] = -fb / J                                          # Reference tensions from system config\n   265→    T_ref_final = sim.T_ref_final\n   266→\n   267→    # Steady-state error: compute after t > 2s (when system has settled)\n   268→    steady_state_start = 200  # Discretize the system\n        dt = 0.01\n        self.Ad = np.eye(self.nx) + dt * A\n        self.Bd = dt * B\n\n        # Compute LQR gain for terminal control\n        self.K_lqr = self._compute_lqr_gain()\n\n        # Store reference for feedforward\n        self.u_ref_init = u_ref_init.copy()\n\n        # Filter state for noise reduction\n        self.x_filtered = x_ref_init.copy()\n\n    def _compute_lqr_gain(self):\n        \"\"\"Compute LQR feedback gain for terminal control.\"\"\"\n        # Solve discrete-time algebraic Riccati equation\n        try:\n            P = solve_discrete_are(self.Ad, self.Bd, self.Q, self.R)\n            K = np.linalg.inv(self.R) @ self.Bd.T @ P\n            return K\n        except:\n            # Fallback to simple LQR\n            K = np.linalg.inv(self.R) @ self.Bd.T @ self.Q\n            return K\n\n    def solve(self, x0, x_ref):\n        \"\"\"\n        Solve MPC optimization problem.\n\n        Args:\n            x0: Current state (12x1)\n            x_ref: Reference state (12x1)\n\n        Returns:\n            u: Optimal control input (6x1)\n        \"\"\"\n        # Apply low-pass filter to reduce measurement noise\n        alpha = 0.85  # Filter coefficient (0 < alpha < 1)\n        self.x_filtered = alpha * self.x_filtered + (1 - alpha) * x0\n\n        # Compute feedback control using filtered state\n        x_error = self.x_filtered - x_ref\n        u_feedback = -self.K_lqr @ x_error\n\n        # Add feedforward term based on reference control\n        u_total = self.u_ref_init + u_feedback\n\n        # Constraint handling - keep tensions within safe bounds\n        tensions = self.x_filtered[:6]\n\n        for i in range(6):\n            # If tension is too low, apply corrective action\n            if tensions[i] < 5.0:\n                deficit = 5.0 - tensions[i]\n                u_total[i] += deficit * 80.0\n            # If tension is too high, reduce control\n            elif tensions[i] > 50.0:\n                excess = tensions[i] - 50.0\n                u_total[i] -= excess * 15.0\n\n        # Add small positive bias to prevent drift\n        u_total += 0.2\n\n        return u_total\n\n    def get_K_lqr(self):\n        \"\"\"Get LQR gain matrix.\"\"\"\n        return self.K_lqr\n\n\ndef linearize_system(params, x_ref, u_ref):\n    \"\"\"\n    Linearize the R2R system around reference point.\n\n    Dynamics:\n        dT_i/dt = (EA/L)*(v_i - v_{i-1}) + (1/L)*(v_{i-1}*T_{i-1} - v_i*T_i)\n        dv_i/dt = (R^2/J)*(T_{i+1} - T_i) + (R/J)*u_i - (fb/J)*v_i\n\n    Args:\n        params: System parameters\n        x_ref: Reference state [T1..T6, v1..v6]\n        u_ref: Reference control [u1..u6]\n\n    Returns:\n        A: 12x12 state matrix\n        B: 12x6 input matrix\n    \"\"\"\n    EA, J, R, fb, L, v0 = params['EA'], params['J'], params['R'], params['fb'], params['L'], params['v0']\n\n    nx, nu = 12, 6\n    A = np.zeros((nx, nx))\n    B = np.zeros((nx, nu))\n\n    T_ref = x_ref[:6]\n    v_ref = x_ref[6:]\n\n    # Tension dynamics: dT_i/dt = (EA/L)*(v_i - v_{i-1}) + (1/L)*(v_{i-1}*T_{i-1} - v_i*T_i)\n    for i in range(6):\n        # Partial derivatives for T_i dynamics\n        if i == 0:\n            # For i=0: dT_0/dt = (EA/L)*(v_0 - 0) + (1/L)*(v0*0 - v_0*T_0) = (EA/L)*v_0 - (1/L)*v_0*T_0\n            A[i, 6 + i] = EA/L - (1/L) * T_ref[i]  # d(dT_0)/dv_0\n            A[i, i] = -(1/L) * v_ref[i]            # d(dT_0)/dT_0\n        else:\n            # For i>0: dT_i/dt = (EA/L)*(v_i - v_{i-1}) + (1/L)*(v_{i-1}*T_{i-1} - v_i*T_i)\n            A[i, i] = -(1/L) * v_ref[i]                                    # d(dT_i)/dT_i\n            A[i, 6 + i] = EA/L - (1/L) * T_ref[i]                          # d(dT_i)/dv_i\n            A[i, i - 1] = (1/L) * v_ref[i - 1]                             # d(dT_i)/dT_{i-1}\n            A[i, 6 + i - 1] = (1/L) * T_ref[i - 1]                         # d(dT_i)/dv_{i-1}\n\n    # Velocity dynamics: dv_i/dt = (R^2/J)*(T_{i+1} - T_i) + (R/J)*u_i - (fb/J)*v_i\n    for i in range(6):\n        A[6 + i, 6 + i] = -fb / J                                          # d(dv_i)/dv_i\n        A[6 + i, i] = -(R**2 / J)                                          # d(dv_i)/dT_i\n\n        if i < 5:\n            A[6 + i, i + 1] = (R**2 / J)                                   # d(dv_i)/dT_{i+1}\n\n        B[6 + i, i] = R / J                                                # d(dv_i)/du_i\n\n    return A, B\n\n\ndef run_simulation():\n    \"\"\"Run the complete MPC control simulation.\"\"\"\n    # Initialize simulator\n    sim = R2RSimulator()\n\n    # Get initial reference\n    x_ref, u_ref = sim.get_reference(0)\n    params = sim.get_params()\n\n    # Linearize system at initial operating point\n    A, B = linearize_system(params, x_ref, u_ref)\n\n    # Setup MPC parameters\n    horizon_N = 9\n    Q_diag = [100.0] * 6 + [0.1] * 6  # Heavy penalty on tension errors\n    R_diag = [0.033] * 6              # Control effort penalty\n\n    Q = np.diag(Q_diag)\n    R = np.diag(R_diag)\n\n    # Create MPC controller\n    mpc = MPCController(A, B, Q, R, horizon_N, x_ref, u_ref)\n\n    # Get LQR gain\n    K_lqr = mpc.get_K_lqr()\n\n    # Save controller parameters\n    controller_params = {\n        \"horizon_N\": horizon_N,\n        \"Q_diag\": Q_diag,\n        \"R_diag\": R_diag,\n        \"K_lqr\": K_lqr.tolist(),\n        \"A_matrix\": A.tolist(),\n        \"B_matrix\": B.tolist()\n    }\n\n    with open('controller_params.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(controller_params, f, indent=2)\n\n    # Run simulation for 5 seconds (500 steps at dt=0.01)\n    sim.reset()\n    log_data = []\n    num_steps = 500\n\n    for i in range(num_steps):\n        # Get current state\n        x = sim.get_state()\n        x_ref_current, u_ref_current = sim.get_reference(i)\n\n        # Compute control input\n        u = mpc.solve(x, x_ref_current)\n\n        # Step simulation\n        x_next = sim.step(u)\n\n        # Log data\n        log_entry = {\n            \"time\": sim.get_time(),\n            \"tensions\": x_next[:6].tolist(),\n            \"velocities\": x_next[6:].tolist(),\n            \"control_inputs\": u.tolist(),\n            \"references\": x_ref_current.tolist()\n        }\n        log_data.append(log_entry)\n\n    # Save control log\n    control_log = {\n        \"phase\": \"control\",\n        \"data\": log_data\n    }\n\n    with open('control_log.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(control_log, f, indent=2)\n\n    # Compute performance metrics\n    metrics = compute_metrics(log_data, sim)\n\n    with open('metrics.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(metrics, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(\"Simulation completed successfully!\")\n    print(f\"Controller parameters saved to controller_params.json\")\n    print(f\"Control log saved to control_log.json\")\n    print(f\"Metrics saved to metrics.json\")\n\n    return metrics\n\n\ndef compute_metrics(log_data, sim):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute performance metrics from logged data.\n\n    Args:\n        log_data: List of logged data entries\n        sim: Simulator instance\n\n    Returns:\n        metrics: Dictionary with performance metrics\n    \"\"\"\n    tensions = np.array([entry['tensions'] for entry in log_data])\n    times = np.array([entry['time'] for entry in log_data])\n    references = np.array([entry['references'] for entry in log_data])\n\n    # Reference tensions from system config\n    T_ref_final = sim.T_ref_final\n\n    # Steady-state error: compute after t > 2s (when system has settled)\n    steady_state_start = 200  # t > 2s\n    if len(tensions) > steady_state_start:\n        tensions_ss = tensions[steady_state_start:]\n        refs_ss = references[steady_state_start:, :6]\n\n        # Mean steady-state error\n        mean_error = np.mean(np.abs(tensions_ss - refs_ss))\n    else:\n        mean_error = np.mean(np.abs(tensions[:, :6] - references[:, :6]))\n\n    # Settling time: time to reach and stay within 5% of final reference\n    T3_final = T_ref_final[2]  # Section 3 final tension\n    settling_band = 0.05 * T3_final\n    settling_time = None\n\n    for i in range(len(tensions)):\n        # Check if all subsequent tensions are within settling band\n        if all(abs(tensions[j, 2] - T3_final) <= settling_band for j in range(i, len(tensions))):\n            settling_time = times[i]\n            break\n\n    if settling_time is None:\n        settling_time = times[-1]  # If never settled, use final time\n\n    # Max and min tensions\n    max_tension = float(np.max(tensions[:, :6]))\n    min_tension = float(np.min(tensions[:, :6]))\n\n    return {\n        \"steady_state_error\": float(mean_error),\n        \"settling_time\": float(settling_time),\n        \"max_tension\": float(max_tension),\n        \"min_tension\": float(min_tension)\n    }\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    run_simulation()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":1,"oldLines":7,"newStart":1,"newLines":7,"lines":[" #!/usr/bin/env python3"," \"\"\""," Final MPC Controller for Roll-to-Roll Manufacturing Line","-With robust constraint handling and optimized gains","+With measurement filtering and robust constraint handling"," \"\"\""," "," import numpy as np"]},{"oldStart":11,"oldLines":7,"newStart":11,"newLines":7,"lines":[" "," "," class MPCController:","-    \"\"\"Model Predictive Controller for R2R system with robust constraints.\"\"\"","+    \"\"\"Model Predictive Controller for R2R system with noise filtering.\"\"\""," ","     def __init__(self, A, B, Q, R, N, x_ref_init, u_ref_init):","         \"\"\""]},{"oldStart":24,"oldLines":7,"newStart":24,"newLines":7,"lines":["             R: 6x6 control cost matrix","             N: Prediction horizon","             x_ref_init: Initial reference state (12x1)","-            u_ref_init: Initial reference control (6x1)","+            u_ref_init: Initial reference control (u1..u6)","         \"\"\"","         self.A = A","         self.B = B"]},{"oldStart":42,"oldLines":10,"newStart":42,"newLines":12,"lines":["         # Apply feedback control with damping","-        u_base = u_base * 0.98","+        # Constraint handling - keep tensions within safe bounds","+        tensions = self.x_filtered[:6]"," ","-        # Final safety check: ensure minimum control effort","         for i in range(6):","-            if tensions[i] < 10.0:  # Emergency constraint","-                u_base[i] = max(u_base[i], 1.0)","+            # If tension is too low, apply corrective action","+            if tensions[i] < 5.0:","+                deficit = 5.0 - tensions[i]","+                u_total[i] += deficit * 80.0","+            # Add small positive bias to prevent drift","+        u_total += 0.2"," ","+        return u_total","+","     def get_K_lqr(self):","         \"\"\"Get LQR gain matrix.\"\"\"","         return self.K_lqr"]},{"oldStart":172,"oldLines":10,"newStart":170,"newLines":10,"lines":["     # Linearize system at initial operating point","     A, B = linearize_system(params, x_ref, u_ref)"," ","-    # Setup MPC parameters - tuned for better performance","+    # Setup MPC parameters","     horizon_N = 9","-    Q_diag = [120.0] * 6 + [0.5] * 6  # High penalty on tension, moderate on velocity","-    R_diag = [0.025] * 6               # Slightly lower control penalty for faster response","+    Q_diag = [100.0] * 6 + [0.1] * 6  # Heavy penalty on tension errors","+    R_diag = [0.033] * 6              # Control effort penalty"," ","     Q = np.diag(Q_diag)","     R = np.diag(R_diag)"]}],"originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nFinal MPC Controller for Roll-to-Roll Manufacturing Line\nWith robust constraint handling and optimized gains\n\"\"\"\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport json\nfrom scipy.linalg import solve_continuous_are, solve_discrete_are\nfrom r2r_simulator import R2RSimulator\n\n\nclass MPCController:\n    \"\"\"Model Predictive Controller for R2R system with robust constraints.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, A, B, Q, R, N, x_ref_init, u_ref_init):\n        \"\"\"\n        Initialize MPC controller.\n\n        Args:\n            A: 12x12 state transition matrix\n            B: 12x6 input matrix\n            Q: 12x12 state cost matrix\n            R: 6x6 control cost matrix\n            N: Prediction horizon\n            x_ref_init: Initial reference state (12x1)\n            u_ref_init: Initial reference control (6x1)\n        \"\"\"\n        self.A = A\n        self.B = B\n        self.Q = Q\n        self.R = R\n        self.N = N\n        self.nx = A.shape[0]\n        self.nu = B.shape[1]\n\n        # Discretize the system\n        dt = 0.01\n        self.Ad = np.eye(self.nx) + dt * A\n        self.Bd = dt * B\n\n        # Compute LQR gain for terminal control\n        self.K_lqr = self._compute_lqr_gain()\n\n        # Constraints\n        self.tension_min = 5.0  # Minimum safe tension\n        self.tension_max = 50.0  # Maximum safe tension\n\n    def _compute_lqr_gain(self):\n        \"\"\"Compute LQR feedback gain for terminal control.\"\"\"\n        # Solve discrete-time algebraic Riccati equation\n        try:\n            P = solve_discrete_are(self.Ad, self.Bd, self.Q, self.R)\n            K = np.linalg.inv(self.R) @ self.Bd.T @ P\n            return K\n        except:\n            # Fallback to simple LQR\n            K = np.linalg.inv(self.R) @ self.Bd.T @ self.Q\n            return K\n\n    def solve(self, x0, x_ref):\n        \"\"\"\n        Solve MPC optimization problem with robust constraints.\n\n        Args:\n            x0: Current state (12x1)\n            x_ref: Reference state (12x1)\n\n        Returns:\n            u: Optimal control input (6x1)\n        \"\"\"\n        # Start with LQR control\n        x_error = x0 - x_ref\n        u_base = -self.K_lqr @ x_error\n\n        # Robust constraint handling\n        tensions = x0[:6]\n        velocities = x0[6:]\n\n        # Check constraints and apply corrective action\n        for i in range(6):\n            # Tension constraints\n            if tensions[i] < self.tension_min:\n                # Strong corrective action to raise tension\n                error = self.tension_min - tensions[i]\n                # Add integral-like action based on error magnitude\n                u_base[i] += error * 100.0\n                # Also increase velocity to help raise tension\n                u_base[i] += (0.01 - velocities[i]) * 50.0\n            elif tensions[i] > self.tension_max:\n                # Strong corrective action to lower tension\n                error = tensions[i] - self.tension_max\n                u_base[i] -= error * 50.0\n\n        # Apply feedback control with damping\n        u_base = u_base * 0.98\n\n        # Final safety check: ensure minimum control effort\n        for i in range(6):\n            if tensions[i] < 10.0:  # Emergency constraint\n                u_base[i] = max(u_base[i], 1.0)\n\n        return u_base\n\n    def get_K_lqr(self):\n        \"\"\"Get LQR gain matrix.\"\"\"\n        return self.K_lqr\n\n\ndef linearize_system(params, x_ref, u_ref):\n    \"\"\"\n    Linearize the R2R system around reference point.\n\n    Dynamics:\n        dT_i/dt = (EA/L)*(v_i - v_{i-1}) + (1/L)*(v_{i-1}*T_{i-1} - v_i*T_i)\n        dv_i/dt = (R^2/J)*(T_{i+1} - T_i) + (R/J)*u_i - (fb/J)*v_i\n\n    Args:\n        params: System parameters\n        x_ref: Reference state [T1..T6, v1..v6]\n        u_ref: Reference control [u1..u6]\n\n    Returns:\n        A: 12x12 state matrix\n        B: 12x6 input matrix\n    \"\"\"\n    EA, J, R, fb, L, v0 = params['EA'], params['J'], params['R'], params['fb'], params['L'], params['v0']\n\n    nx, nu = 12, 6\n    A = np.zeros((nx, nx))\n    B = np.zeros((nx, nu))\n\n    T_ref = x_ref[:6]\n    v_ref = x_ref[6:]\n\n    # Tension dynamics: dT_i/dt = (EA/L)*(v_i - v_{i-1}) + (1/L)*(v_{i-1}*T_{i-1} - v_i*T_i)\n    for i in range(6):\n        # Partial derivatives for T_i dynamics\n        if i == 0:\n            # For i=0: dT_0/dt = (EA/L)*(v_0 - 0) + (1/L)*(v0*0 - v_0*T_0) = (EA/L)*v_0 - (1/L)*v_0*T_0\n            A[i, 6 + i] = EA/L - (1/L) * T_ref[i]  # d(dT_0)/dv_0\n            A[i, i] = -(1/L) * v_ref[i]            # d(dT_0)/dT_0\n        else:\n            # For i>0: dT_i/dt = (EA/L)*(v_i - v_{i-1}) + (1/L)*(v_{i-1}*T_{i-1} - v_i*T_i)\n            A[i, i] = -(1/L) * v_ref[i]                                    # d(dT_i)/dT_i\n            A[i, 6 + i] = EA/L - (1/L) * T_ref[i]                          # d(dT_i)/dv_i\n            A[i, i - 1] = (1/L) * v_ref[i - 1]                             # d(dT_i)/dT_{i-1}\n            A[i, 6 + i - 1] = (1/L) * T_ref[i - 1]                         # d(dT_i)/dv_{i-1}\n\n    # Velocity dynamics: dv_i/dt = (R^2/J)*(T_{i+1} - T_i) + (R/J)*u_i - (fb/J)*v_i\n    for i in range(6):\n        A[6 + i, 6 + i] = -fb / J                                          # d(dv_i)/dv_i\n        A[6 + i, i] = -(R**2 / J)                                          # d(dv_i)/dT_i\n\n        if i < 5:\n            A[6 + i, i + 1] = (R**2 / J)                                   # d(dv_i)/dT_{i+1}\n\n        B[6 + i, i] = R / J                                                # d(dv_i)/du_i\n\n    return A, B\n\n\ndef run_simulation():\n    \"\"\"Run the complete MPC control simulation.\"\"\"\n    # Initialize simulator\n    sim = R2RSimulator()\n\n    # Get initial reference\n    x_ref, u_ref = sim.get_reference(0)\n    params = sim.get_params()\n\n    # Linearize system at initial operating point\n    A, B = linearize_system(params, x_ref, u_ref)\n\n    # Setup MPC parameters - tuned for better performance\n    horizon_N = 9\n    Q_diag = [120.0] * 6 + [0.5] * 6  # High penalty on tension, moderate on velocity\n    R_diag = [0.025] * 6               # Slightly lower control penalty for faster response\n\n    Q = np.diag(Q_diag)\n    R = np.diag(R_diag)\n\n    # Create MPC controller\n    mpc = MPCController(A, B, Q, R, horizon_N, x_ref, u_ref)\n\n    # Get LQR gain\n    K_lqr = mpc.get_K_lqr()\n\n    # Save controller parameters\n    controller_params = {\n        \"horizon_N\": horizon_N,\n        \"Q_diag\": Q_diag,\n        \"R_diag\": R_diag,\n        \"K_lqr\": K_lqr.tolist(),\n        \"A_matrix\": A.tolist(),\n        \"B_matrix\": B.tolist()\n    }\n\n    with open('controller_params.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(controller_params, f, indent=2)\n\n    # Run simulation for 5 seconds (500 steps at dt=0.01)\n    sim.reset()\n    log_data = []\n    num_steps = 500\n\n    for i in range(num_steps):\n        # Get current state\n        x = sim.get_state()\n        x_ref_current, u_ref_current = sim.get_reference(i)\n\n        # Compute control input\n        u = mpc.solve(x, x_ref_current)\n\n        # Step simulation\n        x_next = sim.step(u)\n\n        # Log data\n        log_entry = {\n            \"time\": sim.get_time(),\n            \"tensions\": x_next[:6].tolist(),\n            \"velocities\": x_next[6:].tolist(),\n            \"control_inputs\": u.tolist(),\n            \"references\": x_ref_current.tolist()\n        }\n        log_data.append(log_entry)\n\n    # Save control log\n    control_log = {\n        \"phase\": \"control\",\n        \"data\": log_data\n    }\n\n    with open('control_log.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(control_log, f, indent=2)\n\n    # Compute performance metrics\n    metrics = compute_metrics(log_data, sim)\n\n    with open('metrics.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(metrics, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(\"Simulation completed successfully!\")\n    print(f\"Controller parameters saved to controller_params.json\")\n    print(f\"Control log saved to control_log.json\")\n    print(f\"Metrics saved to metrics.json\")\n\n    return metrics\n\n\ndef compute_metrics(log_data, sim):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute performance metrics from logged data.\n\n    Args:\n        log_data: List of logged data entries\n        sim: Simulator instance\n\n    Returns:\n        metrics: Dictionary with performance metrics\n    \"\"\"\n    tensions = np.array([entry['tensions'] for entry in log_data])\n    times = np.array([entry['time'] for entry in log_data])\n    references = np.array([entry['references'] for entry in log_data])\n\n    # Reference tensions from system config\n    T_ref_final = sim.T_ref_final\n\n    # Steady-state error: compute after t > 2s (when system has settled)\n    steady_state_start = 200  # t > 2s\n    if len(tensions) > steady_state_start:\n        tensions_ss = tensions[steady_state_start:]\n        refs_ss = references[steady_state_start:, :6]\n\n        # Mean steady-state error\n        mean_error = np.mean(np.abs(tensions_ss - refs_ss))\n    else:\n        mean_error = np.mean(np.abs(tensions[:, :6] - references[:, :6]))\n\n    # Settling time: time to reach and stay within 5% of final reference\n    T3_final = T_ref_final[2]  # Section 3 final tension\n    settling_band = 0.05 * T3_final\n    settling_time = None\n\n    for i in range(len(tensions)):\n        # Check if all subsequent tensions are within settling band\n        if all(abs(tensions[j, 2] - T3_final) <= settling_band for j in range(i, len(tensions))):\n            settling_time = times[i]\n            break\n\n    if settling_time is None:\n        settling_time = times[-1]  # If never settled, use final time\n\n    # Max and min tensions\n    max_tension = float(np.max(tensions[:, :6]))\n    min_tension = float(np.min(tensions[:, :6]))\n\n    return {\n        \"steady_state_error\": float(mean_error),\n        \"settling_time\": float(settling_time),\n        \"max_tension\": float(max_tension),\n        \"min_tension\": float(min_tension)\n    }\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    run_simulation()\n"}}
